Each step ends with an `MPI_Allgatherv` of positions, so every rank holds the full state.\n- **OpenMP** runs one host thread per local GPU. Each thread drives its own device, which lets one rank use all the GPUs on its node.\n- **CUDA** kernels compute forces, integrate, and compute potential energy. The force loop keeps the original ascending-`j` summation order per body, so the dynamics stay equivalent to the serial code.\n\n`nbody.cpp` has to be compiled as CUDA so it can hold the kernels, since I can't add new files. Now I'll write the program."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":910,"cache_read_input_tokens":42712,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":910},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f15ffe2b-72c8-482e-8b3b-fd595ee61921","uuid":"1113a014-1c2a-4cb0-90a6-e011fbdc8a42","timestamp":"2026-10-14T10:45:38.073Z","request_id":"req_011Cg1zSAXB6HHxiTv5g1uEz"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg1zSApXm5M5jv7ghPeY8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ByeUSgfE52DgwPL83YZxcw","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261012-111947/nbody_claude-haiku-5.5-cc-medium_hybrid_r5/nbody/nbody.cpp","content":"// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA n-body benchmark.\n//   MPI    : bodies are block-partitioned across ranks; positions are\n//            allgathered after every step so each rank holds the full state.\n//   OpenMP : one host thread per local GPU; each thread drives its own device.\n//   CUDA   : force, integration and energy kernels run on every GPU.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                  \\\n    do {                                                                                  \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                  \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,              \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                            \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                                 \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Body state is stored as flat arrays holding x, y, z for each body.\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<double>& pos, std::vector<double>& vel, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    const size_t n = pos.size() / 3;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            pos[3 * i + c] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        }\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            vel[3 * i + c] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// One thread per locally owned body. Each thread accumulates the interaction with\n// j = 0..n-1 in ascending order, matching the serial summation order. Positions are\n// staged through shared memory in tiles, which does not change that order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ pos, int n,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vel, int lo, int count) {\n    __shared__ double tile[BLOCK_SIZE * 3];\n\n    const int k = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = k < count;\n\n    double xi = 0.0, yi = 0.0, zi = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        const int i = lo + k;\n        xi = pos[3 * i];\n        yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n    }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int base = 0; base < n; base += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int j = base + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) {\n            tile[3 * threadIdx.x] = pos[3 * j];\n            tile[3 * threadIdx.x + 1] = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            tile[3 * threadIdx.x + 2] = pos[3 * j + 2];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const int tileCount = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - base);\n            for (int t = 0; t < tileCount; ++t) {\n                const double dx = tile[3 * t] - xi;\n                const double dy = tile[3 * t + 1] - yi;\n                const double dz = tile[3 * t + 2] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vel[3 * k] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * k + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * k + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ pos, const double* __restrict__ vel,\n                                int lo, int count) {\n    const int k = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    if (k < count) {\n        double* p = pos + 3 * (lo + k);\n        p[0] += vel[3 * k] * DT;\n        p[1] += vel[3 * k + 1] * DT;\n        p[2] += vel[3 * k + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Per-body partial potential energy over pairs (i, j) with j > i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ pos, int n, int lo, int count,\n                                double* __restrict__ partial) {\n    const int k = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    if (k >= count) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int i = lo + k;\n    const double xi = pos[3 * i];\n    const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n    const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = pos[3 * j] - xi;\n        const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n        const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[k] = energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns nullptr if the state is valid, otherwise a description of the first failure.\nconst char* validateSimulation(const std::vector<double>& pos, const std::vector<double>& vel) {\n    const size_t n = pos.size() / 3;\n    const double maxPos = 1e6;\n    const double maxVel = 1e6;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double* p = &pos[3 * i];\n        const double* v = &vel[3 * i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(p[0]) || !std::isfinite(p[1]) || !std::isfinite(p[2]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(v[0]) || !std::isfinite(v[1]) || !std::isfinite(v[2])) {\n            return \"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\";\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        if (std::abs(p[0]) > maxPos || std::abs(p[1]) > maxPos || std::abs(p[2]) > maxPos) {\n            return \"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\";\n        }\n        if (std::abs(v[0]) > maxVel || std::abs(v[1]) > maxVel || std::abs(v[2]) > maxVel) {\n            return \"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\";\n        }\n    }\n    return nullptr;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// The slice of this rank's bodies handled by one GPU (and one OpenMP thread).\nstruct GpuSlice {\n    int device = 0;\n    int lo = 0;                  // first global body index of the slice\n    int count = 0;               // number of bodies in the slice\n    double* pos = nullptr;       // device copy of all positions (3 * numBodies)\n    double* vel = nullptr;       // device velocities of the slice (3 * count)\n    double* partial = nullptr;   // device per-body potential partial sums (count)\n};\n\nstatic int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, int rank, int numRanks) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; positions are\n//            allgathered after every step so each rank holds the full state.\n//   OpenMP : one host thread per local GPU; each thread drives its own device.\n//   CUDA   : force, integration and energy kernels run on every GPU.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                  \\\n    do {                                                                                  \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                  \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,              \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_)); i < n; ++i) {\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            pos[3 * i + c] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        }\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            vel[3 * i + c] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// One thread per locally owned body. Each thread accumulates the interaction with\n// j = 0..n-1 in ascending order, matching the serial summation order. Positions are\n// staged through shared memory in tiles, which does not change that order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ pos, int n,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vel, int lo, int count) {\n    __shared__ double tile[BLOCK_SIZE * 3];\n\n    const int k = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = k < count;\n\n    double xi = 0.0, yi = 0.0, zi = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        const int i = lo + k;\n        xi = pos[3 * i];\n        yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n    }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int base = 0; base < n; base += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int j = base + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) {\n            tile[3 * threadIdx.x] = pos[3 * j];\n            tile[3 * threadIdx.x + 1] = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            tile[3 * threadIdx.x + 2] = pos[3 * j + 2];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const int tileCount = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - base);\n            for (int t = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = pos[3 * j] - xi;\n        const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n        const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[k] = energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values.\n// Returns nullptr if the state is valid, otherwise a description of the first failure.\nconst char* validateSimulation(const std::vector<double>& pos, const std::vector<double>& vel) {\n    const size_t n = pos.size() / 3;\n    const double maxPos = 1e6;\n    const double maxVel = 1e6;\n    for (size_t i = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg21HbgcxSrqajZAU6VUk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program. It builds without warnings, and its output matches the original.\n\n**What changed** (only `nbody/nbody.cpp` and `nbody/CMakeLists.txt`; the executable is still `nbody`, and no files were added):\n\n- **MPI** splits the bodies into contiguous blocks, one per rank. After each step an `MPI_Allgatherv` shares the updated positions, so every rank holds the full state.\n- **OpenMP** runs one host thread per local GPU. Each thread drives its own device, so one rank can use all the GPUs on its node. Ranks take devices round-robin.\n- **CUDA** kernels compute forces, integrate, and compute potential energy. The force kernel stages positions through shared memory in tiles. Each body still accumulates over `j = 0..n-1` in ascending order, so the per-body arithmetic is unchanged.\n- **CMake** now declares the CUDA language, finds MPI, OpenMP and CUDA, and builds `nbody.cpp` with nvcc, forwarding `-O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra -fopenmp` to the host compiler.\n\n**Verification**\n\n- **Bit-exact with contraction off:** with FMA contraction disabled in both builds, the hybrid output matches the original exactly, e.g. hash `37bf5bbdacd6cae0` for 1024 bodies, 10 steps.\n- **Rank counts:** I checked 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, and 8 ranks against the original on body counts of 0, 1, 2, 3, 257, 513, 1000, 1024, and 4096, with 0–10 steps. All matched.\n- **Default build:** the default build uses FMA, so its hashes differ from the original at rounding level (about 1e-11 relative after 10 steps).\n- **Validation and CLI:** `-v` passes on 1 and 2 ranks, and `-h` and unknown options behave as before.\n- **Timing:** at N=16384 and 5 steps, the original takes about 9.9 s and the hybrid about 0.2 s. At N=65536 and 1 step, one GPU takes 492 ms and four GPUs take 168 ms.\n\n**Choices and caveats**\n\n- I kept the per-body summation order so results stay equivalent. The cost is that parallelism inside one body's force sum is not exploited.\n- The energy sum runs in a different order, so the `%.6f` energy can differ in the last digit.\n- Consumer RTX 3090s run FP64 at a fraction of their FP32 rate, so absolute GPU throughput is limited by the double-precision math.\n- A negative `-n` now prints an error and exits 1. The original crashed.\n- The default CUDA architectures are 70, 75, 80, 86 and 90. The build needs CUDA 11.8 or newer for sm_90.\n- I tested on one node with four GPUs and oversubscribed ranks, not on a multi-node cluster.\n- The validation-failure branch is not exercised by any test.\n\nI did not commit anything, since you didn't ask for a commit."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1043,"cache_read_input_tokens":99540,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1043},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f15ffe2b-72c8-482e-8b3b-fd595ee61921","uuid":"48c9cbe7-6904-4fcd-8662-31f5484fbe3a","timestamp":"2026-10-14T10:54:20.660Z","request_id":"req_011Cg21HbP1ArC67gCDiM1DP"}
